# S8-01 — master design pass, part 1 (O16)

Status: **built and tested — targeted Vite DEV fixture and offline verification; Hyperagent rescoring pending**. The committed first-pass sections below are historical. Latest targeted verification and affected capture paths are under “Segmented labels and remaining macOS phone fold follow-up”; the preceding full-matrix evidence remains under “Phone fold follow-up after coordinator macOS replay”; “Vite DEV startup follow-up” records the prior DEV remediation; earlier remediation dispositions are retained below. Scope: Machines / station overview, Equipment model and schematic / alarms, and Fault History. The owner's master prompt and `designprompt1.md` guided the audit, task hierarchy, implementation, screenshot refinement and verification. No deployment, connected historian, physical validation, operator acceptance or Hyperagent score is claimed. The coordinator's two-reviewer 95+ gate remains pending.

## Historical first-pass implementation and audit

| Screen | Operator task and original audit | Resulting hierarchy and refinement |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Machines / station explorer | Select a physical location, identify a machine needing attention, and retain origin on drill-down. Identity columns preceded condition; totals/tree consumed laptop space; there was no scoped quality summary. | Location context and sourced counts, then loaded-page quality / read time, then filters and operational columns. Page counts are never extrapolated to the station. Unknown counts remain Unavailable; stale signals, no current data and unknown condition stay distinct. Record read time names the browser zone; plant timezone and unavailable operating facts are explicit. |
| Equipment model | Select a position and inspect its exact records. The reference caveat was below the interaction; dark mode retained a white studio; the inspector layout was based on viewport width. | Leading reference applicability, existing model/camera/keyboard controls, component list and exact inspector. Native theme tokens color the existing renderer. The actual content container governs columns: inspector below at 1100 with the native rail, alongside at 1440/1920. Selection, modes, reset, focus and drill-down remain native. Geometry is unverified; there is no motion. |
| Equipment schematic / alarms | Read reported pump state and usable values, distinguish a breached reading from a raised alarm, and investigate the event. Static arrows / running-derived pipe colors implied unmeasured flow; alarm state was far to the right. | Reference caveat and availability facts lead. Discharge flow uses only a usable good numeric reading (including real zero), with its source unit. Direction and communications remain unavailable; pipes are neutral. Alarm state is the second column; acknowledgement remains active and retains severity. Viewer mutation actions are gated. |
| Fault History | Find the occurrence, its exact state/reason and retained evidence, then inspect source history. Rollups preceded occurrences and clamped reasons could conceal closure without a clearing reading. | Occurrences and independent retained evidence lead; native maintenance rollups follow. Recorded resolution note, active/acknowledged/resolved distinctions and the canonical occurrence remain visible. A labelled ±30 minute recorded-clock context opens native Performance through existing bounded reads and preserves Machines origin. Phone card headers wrap the Critical badge. |

Per-surface audits, tasks and missing facts are retained under `data/devscratch/r6-s8/S8-01/{machines,equipment-model,equipment-schematic,fault-history}/audit.md`. Health and Performance UI implementation files were not changed. Shared investigation links and existing destination panels were exercised with fixtures.

## Review findings and dispositions

| # | Finding and root fix | Regression evidence |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | AlarmTable warning badges had insufficient small-text contrast. Severity remains an amber/red/neutral container; text now uses native `--mantine-color-text`. Dismissed and defensive unavailable states are readable too. | Browser computed-style foreground/background contrast (with alpha compositing through striped rows) must be at least 4.5:1 for open/acknowledged warning and critical, missing severity, dismissed, unknown and not-yet-raised states, light/dark, desktop/phone. |
| 2 | Operational bands used a hover background and dimmed honesty caveats. Bands now use `--mantine-color-body` with the accent border. Required caveats use native text color at sm; the Reference view outline badge uses native text color. | Computed contrast >= 4.5:1 and caveat font >= 14 px across Machines, Equipment and Fault History; band background equality with the native body is asserted. |
| 3 | View-only users saw mutation actions. Acknowledge / Dismiss now require `hasChangeRole(UserRoles.work_order)`; Create repair requires `hasAddRole`. Backend semantics and enforcement are unchanged. | Viewer, editor, change-only and add-only cases on desktop/phone. Read links remain available; acknowledged rows have no second acknowledge. Existing note modal behavior remains: acknowledgement note optional, dismissal reason required. |
| 4 | The location disclosure used 75em while the app uses lg 74em, and bare harness providers omitted app theme / rail width. The disclosure reads `useMantineTheme().breakpoints.lg`; Grid and visibility share that boundary. Captures exposed Mantine overriding HTML hidden, so visibility uses an explicit display rule. S8/S2 use the actual ThemeContext and native PanelGroup rail. Equipment columns use actual available width. | 1183/1184/1190/1199/1200 px assertions verify tree/disclosure alignment and containment. S2 preserves >=560 px model width, list placement and overflow assertions; 1100 now restores the original inspector-below expectation within the real rail. S2 fixture URLs now assert the canonical machine route instead of `/fixture`. |
| 5 | Selected model marker white text lacked contrast on the default primary fill. The marker now uses native primary shade 8 with `--mantine-primary-color-contrast`. | Computed contrast >=4.5:1 for selected visible motor markers, both indigo and blue preferences, light/dark, desktop/phone. No geometry or animation was added. |
| 6 | The process availability Group had an accessible name without a grouping role. It now renders a borderless native fieldset (implicit group) with that name. | Browser `getByRole('group', name='Process data availability')` assertion; host accessibility lint passes without a waiver. |
| 7 | Capture replay overwrote inspected evidence. Default capture destinations now use `testInfo.outputPath()`. Export requires explicit `AIMMS_CAPTURE_BATCH` with a bounded batch name. Original before/after and inspected manifests are retained. | A normal, non-export replay passed and left **180 existing evidence PNG hashes unchanged**, while producing one fresh capture in its test output directory. Final matrix/state exports use new batch directories. |
| 8 | Observed-at values lacked a display zone. AlarmTable now labels the actual IANA zone, preferring a valid supplied `AIMMS_PLANT_TIMEZONE` setting and otherwise the browser zone. Invalid timestamps say Time unavailable. | Four pure unit cases: browser fallback, configured plant conversion, invalid zone fallback, invalid time. Browser cases verify Asia/Riyadh fallback and supplied America/Chicago. The normal fixture does not pretend that the absent plant setting exists. |

The first native-theme probe hit the unchanged unexpected-request guard: LanguageContext performs metadata/settings/status/icon bootstrap reads and trace completion. Explicit synthetic fixtures now cover only those known endpoints/methods; the guard still rejects other backend/external requests. That diagnostic run was interrupted after two failures and is not acceptance.

The first combined S8 run had 46 passes / 2 failures because the new theme assertion incorrectly expected light indigo shade 6 in dark mode. The installed native theme defines dark shade 8. The assertion now checks the exact native light/dark values; the affected four-case standalone replay passed. All 48 distinct S8 behavior cases have a passing result across these recorded runs. This was a test expectation correction, not a product color override.

Browser renders and checks use **Linux Chromium in the dedicated `r6-design-browser-15219` Docker container**, ws 15219, harness 15209. Scratch limits are 180 seconds per functional test / 60 seconds per assertion (matrix tests retain their 120-second limit). The original native suite limits remain unchanged. These shared software-renderer functional results are not target-hardware latency acceptance.

The local fixture bundle uses native components, actual ThemeContext / language and modal providers, and the existing PanelGroup tab rail. Unselected fixture destinations are labelled stubs; this is not a full authenticated AppShell or release-build acceptance. English labels are asserted. Development React/Lingui fallback messages are used by the minified fixture build; delivery CI still needs native extraction/compilation and the normal production build.

The earlier pre-review checks and their failures/replays remain in the command inventory and [archived report](../../../../data/devscratch/r6-s8/S8-01/S8-01-pre-review-report.md). Their bare-provider captures are historical comparisons and are superseded for app-theme scoring. In particular, earlier md / 1100 beside-model conclusions are superseded by the native-rail container layout verified here. The deterministic one-read selection test retains its explicit fixture-only polling pause and exact-one-read assertion; product polling remains 30 seconds.

## Historical first-pass captures

There are **124 final original PNGs: 40 core matrix images and 84 state/selection images**. The core covers all four render surfaces at 1100/1440/1920/390, light/dark, plus full phone pages. States cover stale station data, empty station, stale / unknown schematic data, a reported polling connection error with communications unavailable, active / acknowledged / clearing-reading history, and selected-bearing reference views.

All 40 final core originals were opened individually. The 64 state viewports were inspected in eight contact sheets made from the actual PNGs; full phone and selected-bearing images were inspected separately. Two cleared-phone full pages are SHA-256 identical to the already inspected core full pages. `review-inspection.json` records path/hash/observations; [review-final-capture-manifest.json](../../../../data/devscratch/r6-s8/S8-01/review-final-capture-manifest.json) records every final path, dimensions, hash and inspection method. The eight QA contact sheets are comparison aids, not replacement evidence.

The full real-theme matrix passed 32/32. Machines then refreshed all eight cases after the disclosure fix; Fault History refreshed all eight after normalizing overview capture scroll position so the fixture provenance and native rail remain visible. Other rendered source files were unchanged (`review-capture-source-delta.json`). Old PNGs, before/after pairs and their original inspected hashes were preserved.

Final folders: Machines `machines/browser/review-final-v2/`; model core `equipment-model/browser/review-final/` with selected states in `review-final-v2/states/`; schematic core `equipment-schematic/browser/review-final/` with states in `review-final-v2/states/`; Fault History `fault-history/browser/review-final-v2/`. All are beneath `data/devscratch/r6-s8/S8-01/`.

## Missing facts and open delivery items

- The frontend location/context API does not expose the server's `AIMMS_PLANT_TIMEZONE`. Location timezone is not substituted for plant time. Browser record/observation display zones are named; a supplied valid plant setting is supported and tested.
- The Machines list has no telemetry observation timestamps, run/stop projection or physical communication-status fact. Record-read time is not telemetry freshness. Offline / no current data is not called communication loss.
- Mimic polling metadata can report `connection_failed`, but does not establish physical communication loss or measured flow direction. The corresponding capture shows the polling error and honest communication unavailable. A true communication-lost verdict cannot be fabricated from this API.
- Model geometry and draft component assignments remain unverified reference/inference data. No physical, safety, service or healthy outcome is inferred.
- No database was read, no Azure writes / migrations / dry runs / app startup against Azure occurred, and no backend change or Django test was required. No dependency was installed or downloaded. Shared ledgers and other checkouts were not edited; all work remains uncommitted.
- Hyperagent scoring, coordinator review and connected/deployed R6 acceptance remain open.

## CI additions

Discover the S8 design/review/state specs and harness in `playwright.s8.config.ts` / `tsconfig.s8.json`; retain default and every sprint type project. Run the configured unit suite; S8 desktop/phone functional cases; real-theme core/state captures; S0 locations/mimic (including phone discovery); S1 explorer; all S2 Equipment suites; and S3 Fault History. Keep native unexpected-request guards, exact route/scope/read-count assertions, computed contrast checks and independent add/change permission coverage. Archive routine test-output captures without modifying inspected export batches; exercise the hash-protection replay. Use explicit uniquely named export batches for review. Run host `prek run --files` and the normal production Lingui/build pipeline. These fixture checks do not replace P34–P36 connected acceptance.

## Historical first-pass cleanup

The dedicated `r6-design-browser-15219` container was stopped successfully. Preview listener 15209 remains (PID 48462) from the interrupted initial probe: this sandbox denied SIGINT / TERM with `Operation not permitted` (`review-probe-interrupt`, `review-preview-cleanup`). It was reused only for this checkout’s isolated fixture bundle. The coordinator needs to stop that listener from the host. All source changes remain uncommitted.


## Hyperagent round 1 dispositions

Baseline `e243ae5fc`; coordinator-approved reconciled specification (O22). Direct task rules take precedence over the spec's proposed database, login and ledger steps: disposable offline SQLite tests; intercepted synthetic APIs; no app login/database reads; no shared-ledger edits; no git writes. Owner rescoring remains pending.

| Item | Disposition and evidence |
| --- | --- |
| P1 | Added read-only mimic display shift fields and serializer/type fields; status ages always accompany the history-shift disclosure when supplied. Offline zero/positive-shift tests; S8 shifted/unshifted cases. |
| P2 | Added distinct source error/success timestamps; absent timestamps visibly say time not supplied. Poll attempts and payload generation are not substituted. Offline and S8 polling-code checks. |
| P3 | Added nullable plant_timezone on mimic, occurrences and location context from AIMMS_PLANT_TIMEZONE only; removed the settings API/state fiction. Offline null/declared-zone tests and formatter/browser checks. |
| P4 | Added distinct own/child warning annotations and a read-only serializer field. Info remains Other; older payloads use Non-critical. Offline registry tests, page-facts units and info/fallback captures. |
| P5 | Optional server ordering deferred. No sortable header or page-only sort UI shipped. |
| F0 | S8 uses the production five-tag/thirteen-part layout, kW power, a poll attempt and real NETWORK code; coherent own/child/info alarm rows; actual vote/limit metric keys, legacy keys, dismissal record and a coherent default snapshot window. Deliberate outside-span evidence lives in its own state. All values are synthetic. |
| X1 | Shared StatusBadge and alarmStateVisual provide icon/text/variant vocabulary. Open is filled; acknowledged stays active with an outline; resolved and dismissed are grey history states. Unknown severity is unavailable; AlarmTable keeps the recorded severity visible even when the current reading is unavailable. Work priority is an outline flag. Rendered badge/text sweeps cover both schemes. |
| X2 | Scoped metadata classes use gray-7/dark-1; filter labels/descriptions and Any criticality are visible. No Mantine variable override. Source checks and rendered text/placeholder sweeps. |
| X3 | Scoped AIMMS links and subtle actions use primary-7/primary-4. No global anchor override. Rendered checks cover Health, Parts and evidence links. |
| X4 | Native input/switch borders, shade-7 segmented indicators, text-colored hotspot borders and two-tone focus treatment. Non-text computed-style sweeps and keyboard focus sheets. |
| X5 | One shared 24-hour formatter/validated display clock; API-declared plant clock or explicitly labelled browser fallback. Location timezone stays a separate fact. Unit tests cover browser, supplied/invalid zone and invalid timestamps; per-capture clock count and source scan. |
| X6 | Visible unavailable reasons; known zero remains 0; manufacturer/model Not recorded. No standalone dash cells. Unit, browser and source checks. |
| X7 | One compact contextual band per panel; essential reference/review/clock/count/active/resolved/evidence caveats remain visible and constrained to 70ch. Long notes use Spoiler. Final fold captures and measurements. |
| X8 | Labelled sections and native visible accordion names; one operational live output per base panel, row-specific alarm actions, Switch description and disclosure aria-expanded/controls. Selection is announced separately from static model provenance. |
| X9 | Safari-compatible query serialization, native theme lg visibility and actual-content-width model layout, style props/default radius and note Spoiler. Query fallback unit and all TypeScript projects. |
| X10 | Shared freshness renders server-provided age/reason; stale withholds values and uses a grey clock label. Missing timestamp/age are explicit. No client freshness threshold. Age-format and branch units; stale/unknown browser cases. |
| S1 | Status age shares the unit header; usable tags carry age; read/poll attempt are labelled separately; shifted history remains disclosed. |
| S2 | Query errors, polling failures and disabled polling use neutral acquisition alerts, in the requested order before the review note. Real connector codes and distinct attempt/error/success times; cached values/status hidden on query failure. No communication-loss claim. |
| S3 | Stale geometry uses neutral hatching/dashes and a grey clock badge; no withheld value is reconstructed. Stale state/contrast checks. |
| S4 | Pipes and valves stay neutral in all states, with no arrows, motion or inferred flow; a visible state legend explains unavailable direction/valve position. |
| S5 | Plant review is a compact neutral note with a grey outline badge and visible missing-mapping explanation. No equipment-warning Alert. |
| S6 | The SVG itself fits available content width/height, without a large letterbox. Dark panel uses native dark-8. Whole-SVG bounds checked at 1440/1920. |
| S7 | Production five-tag layout, readable labels/values, text-colored leaders and repositioned reference labels avoid overlap while preserving anchor mappings. Capture geometry records font sizes/tag/marker intersections. Tags retain a 600px drawing minimum and fitting uses document position; mapped-part numeral contrast uses the actual SVG circle fill and passes for all four verdicts. Long component names and detail lines use bounded native Text truncation; full strings remain accessible, and all 39 label boxes are checked against their cards. |
| S8 | Process facts are a labelled dt/dd list. Polling reports enabled / the reported error / disabled, rather than inferring a successful completed poll from an empty error code. DischargeFlowReading requires usable, good, finite data including real zero; direction and communications are explicitly unavailable. Table-driven units and browser assertions. |
| S9 | Phone Key readings precedes the 720px contained sideways drawing. Document overflow and first-viewport header checked. |
| S10 | Shared alarm vocabulary, display clock, row-specific native actions and occurrence links; existing server-mirroring change/add role gates retained. Viewer/acknowledger captures include open, acknowledged, dismissed and not-raised rows. |
| H1 | Opened occurrence leads with actual title, severity and state; acknowledgement, resolution time/note and dismissal metadata come from their supplied fields. No inferred cleared state or resolution-reason field. |
| H2 | List precedes inline detail beneath its own row/card, preserving URL/anchor and focus. Focus prevents scroll from hiding the list header. Newest-first count; no sort UI. |
| H3 | One display-clock statement; shifts only when returned. Case-span picker names the same clock. No fixed dashboard-shift prose. |
| H4 | Real detector metrics have precise labels and units only from metrics.unit; null limits omitted; vote fields grouped. Legacy keys render verbatim, backend summary unchanged. Units and real/legacy fixtures. |
| H5 | Retained window/single-sample time, sample age at capture and exact outside-span predicate; grey data-quality flag, retained quality/stale preserved. Units and dedicated outside-span capture. |
| H6 | One visible continuity policy sentence above the list, without inventing gap data; concise investigation caveats and Spoilers. |
| M1 | Exclusive critical/warning/offline/assessed-unknown/unavailable/normal page facts, overlapping stale caption, actual alarm-row count and server has_alarms filter. No station health total or client health filter. |
| M2 | Compact location identity/context with separate location timezone, sourced counts and one display clock; native tree labels truncate with a Tooltip. Fold assertions and captures. |
| M3 | Identity consolidates serial/manufacturer/model/location/inactive record; operational columns remain condition/alarms/work/criticality. 720px minimum and table overflow measurements. |
| M4 | Condition/alarms/offline tooltips explain backend scope; info is not warning; unavailable is distinct from assessed unknown. Keyboard and fixture checks. |
| M5 | Successful empty station has no chips/table/0-of-0 or fabricated health; browse action only, with no invented assignment flow. Viewer empty-state checks. |
| M6 | Native phone cards retain operational facts; Location details and Filters collapse, native tab labels stay on one line. First-card fold and page-overflow assertions. |
| E1 | Reference badge stays pinned inside the model frame; conceptual geometry is unverified. Both-scheme contrast and phone captures. |
| E2 | Whole-machine work count links to native maintenance; component wording replaces slot UI; links to existing Health/Parts remain. Position reading age is unavailable because that API does not provide server-relative age; stale remains explicit even when age is absent. |
| E3 | Native 52em container layout with min560px model/min260px inspector; components follow inspector. Under-threshold containers stack. Scene height uses document position, so selection after scrolling cannot resize it or change Reset. S2 assertions use measured container width, preserving minimum width and overflow checks. |
| E4 | Renderer reports occluded markers; visible caption names hidden parts and updates in Cutaway. No inference that hidden means absent. |
| E5 | Phone View menu, full-width modes, pan-y default and explicit Rotate toggle; menu actions close after activation. Existing camera/selection/keyboard/zoom/reset contracts retained. |
| E6 | Theme-token floor/grid/fallback; React scheme/primary effect calls setTheme; inherited marker font. No MutationObserver or motion. |
| §7 | All refuted/rule-breaking suggestions excluded: no inferred comms/flow/last-good values/clearing/reason/gaps, client sort/health filtering, global theme fork or hidden essential caveats. |
| §8 | Review packet includes prompts, every changed/new product file and test. Full native shell/page/ThemeContext under interception. Native dark PanelGroup title contrast and clipped phone detail rail remain documented host chrome, outside authorized redesign. |
| §9 | Exact commands/counts and failed/interrupted attempts are retained in the command inventory below. Offline runner now mounts checked-in mimic SVGs read-only so existing layout tests run in the disposable workspace. No network, Azure, migration or dependency change. |
| §10 | New immutable named capture batch with viewport/full-page pairs, computed-style contrast/non-text/overflow/fold/clock JSON, alarm elements and labelled contact sheets; previous inspected batches remain intact. |


## Historical Hyperagent round 1 preview verification

All browser renders used Linux Chromium in the task's own `r6-design-browser-15219` container, websocket 15219 and harness 15209. Each Playwright command ran alone in this checkout. This is native React / Mantine rendering under route interception, not connected-plant or deployed-product acceptance. The fixture build uses development React with minified Vite output and English catalogs; no production-build performance claim is made.

**Exact argv, cwd, exit, counts and logs for every validation attempt, including failed and interrupted runs:** `data/devscratch/r6-s8/S8-01/hyper-r1/commands.md` and `commands.jsonl` at the evidence root. There are 368 passing cases in the selected browser checks above; repeated diagnostic / capture refinements are separately recorded, not counted as additional acceptance. Default 45s / 5s runs and isolated 180s / 60s shared-renderer replays are both retained; the latter prove behavior, not latency acceptance.

The broad `machine_health assets` attempt discovered 792 tests and was stopped after 1,234.68s (exit 137) while a migration rehearsal did not progress. It had notification/import/source-fixture errors and has no completed suite result. The first targeted attempt ran 125 tests with 123 passing and two missing-SVG errors. The offline runner now binds the checked-in mimic assets read-only and copies them into its disposable workspace; the corrected targeted run passed all 126. No migration or database model change was needed. Host `ps aux | grep '[m]anage.py test'` was attempted but the sandbox denied process listing; each offline run used a separate container and uniquely mounted SQLite database, avoiding a shared test database.

### Changed expectations and root fixes retained by the checks

- E3's old viewport-only inspector assertion was replaced with the actual 832px content-container boundary, preserving minimum 560px canvas width, inspector order, list placement and no page overflow. A real phone height jump after scroll / selection was fixed using document position; exact Reset pixels and explicit PNG width / height assertions remain.
- Exact model comparisons hide only the pinned HTML reference badge, whose font rasterization changed with scroll. Scene pixels, modes, camera presets, selection, focus, zoom and Reset remain exact. An unsuccessful `toDataURL` diagnostic is retained: WebGL intentionally does not preserve its drawing buffer, so that approach was removed.
- Legacy expectations follow the approved active / acknowledged vocabulary, row-specific link names, explicit freshness, real zero, consolidated columns, Any criticality and native phone disclosures. URL, server filtering / page resets, permissions, mutation bodies, cache/error and drill-down assertions remain. Legacy alarm fixtures now declare warning counts instead of inferring a remainder.
- Native Gantt's two optional remote font fallbacks are fulfilled locally with zero bytes. Every other unexpected backend/external request still fails the guard; no font download or dependency install occurred.
- Visual inspection also caught 1100px tag label/age collisions. Discharge tags were widened within existing reference space, absent observation ages are omitted from the tag (the visible missing-reading/time disclosures remain), and tag text separation/containment is now asserted. The SVG keeps a 600px minimum drawing width for legibility inside its native scroll area, and fitting uses document position. Earlier final batches are retained. Full-size v4 inspection then caught a long component name overrunning its legend card into a tag. Names and both detail lines now use native Mantine Text truncation inside fixed SVG foreignObject bounds; the full strings remain in titles and accessible names. All 39 legend text boxes must remain inside their cards. Mantine 9 type checking rejected a numeric size token; numeric pixels now use the supported fz style prop, followed by a fresh nine-project pass. The preview rescoring batch was v5; the DEV follow-up below supersedes it for dev-server verification.
- Mapped reference-part numerals now use darker native shades and black text for normal and warning. The added test first caught white-on-green at 4.37:1; the fix uses black on green/amber. A duplicate geometry/overlay selector was tightened to require one overlay and both its key/drawing numerals without weakening contrast. A dedicated four-state test checks the actual adjacent SVG-circle fill in both themes / projects; its 16 element crops supplement the matrix. A subsequent native Text test assumed a div even though Text defaults to p; its obsolete browser run was stopped by restarting only the task container and the generic child selector was replayed. The intermediate count assertion incorrectly expected one numeral; the corrected assertion requires both the key and drawing numeral, each checked for contrast. No successful poll is inferred from an empty error code: enabled / reported error / disabled are stated literally, with a no-attempt refresh case.
- The final state sweep caught unit-filter and investigation-picker border failures; both use scoped native input tokens. Fold measurements caught a phone Key readings header and the opened 901 badges just outside the viewport; gaps were tightened while keeping every essential caveat visible.

## Historical Hyperagent preview captures and inspection

The new batch is `hyper-r1-final-v5`: **520 PNGs, all inspected through 62 labelled contact sheets, with native-size base and targeted state originals also inspected; 0 pending inspections and 0 metadata failures.** Another 16 mapped-part crops were inspected on their own labelled sheet. All originals have viewport / full-page pairs, computed text/non-text contrast, page/table overflow measurements, fold geometry and one-clock counts. Alarm row element crops have their own JSON. Native phone drawing/table scrolling is deliberate and contained; document overflow is zero. Within the changed operational panels, text sweeps meet 4.5:1 and measured interactive edges meet 3:1 in both schemes. The documented native host-chrome exceptions below remain outside this scope.

- `data/devscratch/r6-s8/S8-01/hyper-r1/capture-manifest.json`: complete paths, hashes, renderer/mount and inspection records.

- `data/devscratch/r6-s8/S8-01/hyper-r1/contact-sheets/hyper-r1-final-v5/`: labelled screen / state / width / theme sheets, inspected alongside full-size originals and full-page evidence.
- `data/devscratch/r6-s8/S8-01/hyper-r1/code-and-prompts.md`: full prompt/spec and changed/new source/test review packet, including files omitted from the first review packet.
- `data/devscratch/r6-s8/S8-01/{machines,equipment-model,equipment-schematic,fault-history}/audit.md`: operator task, hierarchy, rendered findings and missing facts.

Measured final base folds (both schemes agree): Machines shows 5 complete rows at 1440 and 8 at 1920; the first 390px card ends at y=796.44 within the 844px viewport. The page-facts row is 40px at 1440. The 1100px model canvas is 560px wide with its inspector beside it; Components is visible at 1440. Schematic tag labels measure approximately 11px at 1100 and 12px at 1440. Opened resolved-901 badges end at y=892.80 within the 900px viewport. Detailed measurements are in `fold-summary.json`.

Before/after pairs use the unmodified native-shell `hyper-r1-provisional/base-*` images and final `hyper-r1-final-v5/base-*` images at the same four widths and schemes. Subsequent fold/edge refinement batches are retained. The reviewed baseline manifest verifies all 124 images unchanged. Forty hashes in an older generic after-image manifest already disagreed before baseline e243ae5fc; all forty image mtimes predate the baseline. Those older files/manifests were preserved and are not used as final evidence (`hyper-r1/reviewed-baseline-integrity.json`, `historical-manifest-differences.json`).

### Remaining facts and delivery boundaries

Communication status, flow direction and valve position are not supplied; the UI says Unavailable with its reason. Reference geometry / physical arrangement is unverified and plant review remains pending. The positions API supplies no server-relative reading age or display-shift metadata. Plant time zone is a declared clock only when the approved API field contains a valid zone; otherwise the browser clock is labelled. There is no resolution-reason or gap-detection field. Optional server ordering was deferred and no sort UI exists.

The two-reviewer 95+ Hyperagent gate is pending coordinator/owner rescoring. Native dark PanelGroup title contrast and the clipped phone detail-tab rail are outside this authorized redesign and remain documented host chrome. The broad backend module suite is incomplete as described above. No connected historian, physical validation, deployment or product acceptance is claimed. Health and Performance panel implementation files were not changed; required shared health/investigation helpers need normal coordinator integration review with the parallel streams.

### CI steps to add

Run the eight targeted backend labels with the offline runner and its checked-in SVG mount; run default plus every sprint tsc, units and English extraction/compile; run S0/S1/S2/S3 target specs and S8 behavior on an isolated browser port; export state/focus captures into a unique named batch, assert contrast/overflow/fold/clock/geometry, retain before/after hashes and attach the review packet. Restore source locale catalogs after fixture builds. Resolve broader offline module-suite fixture/migration limitations separately before claiming module-wide acceptance.

## Historical Hyperagent preview cleanup and integrity

At preview closeout, the task browser container `r6-design-browser-15219` was stopped and ports 15209 and 15219 were closed. The historical integrity verification passed: all 520 matrix hashes, 16 supplementary marker hashes, 124 unchanged reviewed-baseline hashes and 897 runtime-source hashes match. All 520 matrix images have inspection records and zero metadata failures. `git diff --check` passed; 82 files remain uncommitted. Shared ledgers, source locale catalogs, dependencies and migrations are untouched. Evidence: `hyper-r1/final-integrity.json` and `hyper-r1-final-integrity.log`.

## Vite DEV startup follow-up

Status: built and tested — requested DEV replay and final affected checks complete. Earlier browser results above used a prebuilt preview bundle and did not establish Vite DEV startup. The coordinator's host replay exposed a real gap in that verification.

### Root cause and disposition

This was an import-evaluation-order error, not a circular import through the new shared clock or status helpers. `MachineDetail → FaultHistoryPanel → OccurrenceInvestigation → InvestigationSlices` evaluates the investigation module even when another native tab is selected. Its plugin initialization statements preceded its `dayjs` import. Vite DEV rewrites that CommonJS default import to a `const dayjs` initializer at the declaration's location: the two preceding `dayjs.extend` statements read the binding in its temporal dead zone. A bundled build hoisted initialization and concealed the defect.

Moved all imports before the UTC/timezone plugin initialization in `InvestigationSlices.tsx`, retaining the UTC-before-timezone extension order and existing plant/browser clock behavior. The local TypeScript import-graph probe found six reachable local modules and no cycle through InvestigationSlices, common.tsx or observedTime.ts. A changed-file scan found a second interleaved import only in the S8 review spec; that test import now also precedes hook registration. The final scan covers 64 changed frontend modules and reports zero imports after top-level expressions.

Added `tests/s0/pui_module_startup.spec.ts` to S0 discovery and tsconfig.s0.json. It loads the real native Machine Details fixture, imports the investigation component through Vite's browser module loader, and requires its export and a native Details field. This explicitly exercises the dev transform and cannot silently substitute the preview bundle. Existing runtime-error and unexpected-request guards remain active.

Evidence at `data/devscratch/r6-s8/S8-01/`: `hyper-r1-dev-reproduce-browser.log` reproduces the exact blank root and dayjs ReferenceError; `hyper-r1-dev-regression-red.log` fails on the same TDZ; `hyper-r1-dev-regression-native.log` passes the final native-fixture regression. `dev-replay/transformed-order.json` records the corrected dev binding at line 6, before plugin calls at lines 18–19. `hyper-r1-dev-import-graph.log` and `hyper-r1-dev-import-order-final.log` record the graph and source-order checks.

The first post-edit probe still received the stale transform from this checkout's Vite watcher; the task's own dev server was restarted. An initial minimal HTML probe then exposed missing React Refresh preamble, so the final regression uses the actual Vite-served native HTML rather than fabricating that bootstrap. Those diagnostic failures and the interrupted pre-restart S0 attempt remain in the command ledger and are not acceptance.

### DEV capture finding and disposition

Reviewing the actual all-condition-unavailable Machines capture found another honesty defect: the summary printed “Stale signals on 0 machines” despite having no condition coverage for 25 machines. MachinePageSummary now uses the existing unavailable-condition count to disclose missing stale-signal coverage. It preserves confirmed positive stale counts and adds the number whose coverage is unavailable; it shows no zero-stale conclusion when any unchecked coverage remains and no stale signal is confirmed. A fully supplied zero remains a known zero. No new API field or domain assumption is needed.

The new S8 browser regression failed on desktop and phone before this change (`hyper-r1-dev-stale-red`: 2 failed) and passes afterward. The mixed known/unknown assertion preserves the existing known count of 10 and requires the missing coverage for one machine to be stated. The condition-unavailable capture case now asserts the same semantics rather than only saving a picture. New text uses Lingui pluralization; English extraction and catalog compilation passed, and source PO bytes were restored unchanged. Production CI must extract/compile current messages before building, as already required for the preceding R6 strings.

The corrected native-theme Machines evidence is v2; the v1 false-zero images are retained as before evidence. Its first phone card ends at **802.47px in an 844px viewport**; five whole rows remain at 1440 and eight at 1920. At 1100 the real Layout fits a **560px canvas beside a 279.67px inspector**, with a 12px gap and no page overflow. The historical first-pass “below at 1100” description applies to the narrower standalone wrapper; placement is governed by the actual content container, not viewport width alone.

### DEV verification and exact commands

All browser commands below use the scratch `dev-browser.config.mts`, whose webServer command is `node node_modules/vite/bin/vite.js --config vite.s0.config.ts` from this checkout's frontend. Harness 15209 connects to **Linux Chromium in the task's r6-design-browser-15219 container**, ws 15219. These are Vite DEV renders, with React Refresh/CommonJS dev transforms, not preview or prebuilt-bundle checks. Every backend/external request is intercepted by the fixtures/guards; no app login or database is used. One Playwright command ran at a time. Native test and assertion timeouts were not changed.

These results establish fixture behavior, not target-hardware latency acceptance.

The full DEV replay established the startup fix. The only subsequent application-source change is the Machines coverage caption; the other differences in the 897-file runtime hash inventory are locally compiled locale outputs. All affected Machines paths were rerun afterward, rather than claiming the earlier full runs had tested that later caption. There are **583 distinct selected browser cases with passing coverage** across the final relevant checks: S0 110, S2 69, S3 228 and S8 176 (174 existing + two new coverage regressions). Repeated replays are not added to that distinct count.

Exact argv, cwd, exits, durations, counts and diagnostic logs for every validation attempt are retained in `data/devscratch/r6-s8/S8-01/hyper-r1/commands.md` and `commands.jsonl`. A focused DEV command ledger is at `dev-replay/commands.md`; browser/server setup and cleanup are recorded in `dev-replay/setup.md`. Read-only searches and image viewing are not test commands. Earlier preview and failed/partial checks remain historical evidence, not deployment or product acceptance.

## Historical Vite DEV closeout integrity before phone-fold follow-up

Final integrity passed: **600 selected PNG hashes**, **897 runtime source hashes**, **124 original reviewed-baseline hashes**, **520 untouched preview-v5 hashes**, and **520 untouched DEV-v1 hashes**. All 600 selected images have inspection records and there are zero selected metadata failures. Final Machines fold checks pass in both schemes (phone first card fully visible; at least five/eight desktop rows at 1440/1920). Evidence: `dev-replay/final-integrity.json`, `hyper-r1-dev-final-integrity.log`, and `dev-replay/final-capture-manifest.json`.

The task's own browser container is absent and ports **15209/15219 are closed**. Host prek passed on all 84 changed files, and git diff --check passed. All changes remain uncommitted on r6/design-pass for coordinator review. Shared ledgers, dependencies, migrations and source PO catalogs remain untouched. The owner’s Hyperagent rescoring gate and product/deployment acceptance remain open.


## Phone fold follow-up after coordinator macOS replay

Status: **built and tested for the targeted Linux Chromium fixture checks**. The coordinator reported six macOS-only fold failures (both schemes): base-machines first-card bottom 889.7px at 390x844, Cutaway measurement 861.8px, and resolved-event measurement 926.4px. This follow-up fixes spacing and hierarchy at their source. Existing assertions and bounds were retained; Cutaway now also receives the existing base-model E3 matrix assertions. All facts, caveats and existing text sizes remain available. No backend, catalog or dependency change was made in this follow-up.

| Screen | Audit and root change | Final measured Linux result, both schemes |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Machines | Location details occupied a separate row, and the closed filter Collapse still occupied a flex gap. Move Location details into the existing location action row, remove the closed Collapse from layout, and compact scoped phone stack/band spacing. Keep page-count, missing stale-coverage, unavailable telemetry/run-stop/communication and clock qualifications. | At 390x844, complete first card bottom **731.00px**, formerly 802.47px in Linux before captures. Wider-font check **739.81px**. Desktop still shows at least five/eight whole rows at 1440/1920. |
| 3D model / Cutaway | The reference band and stacked phone toolbars consumed avoidable space. Compact phone padding, heading leading, stack gaps and the reference-band control gap. Retain every reference label, timestamp and control, plus the Cutaway and occlusion explanations. | Phone scene starts at **713.75px**, formerly 758.83px; wider-font scene starts at **717.95px**, with the first 80px inside 844px. At 1440, component list begins at **770.72px**, **772.13px** under wider metrics, within the existing 900px bound. At 1100 the canvas remains >=560px wide beside its inspector. |
| Fault History | Refresh wrapped into a separate phone row; longer occurrence identity separated the title from its state. Use a grid heading with Refresh at right, compact phone leading/gaps, and group the opened title with severity/state before identity. Retain all three caveats and the full resolution explanation. | At 1440, opened resolved-state bottom **838.20px**, formerly 892.80px; wider metrics **874.41px**, within the unchanged 900px bound. Phone first occurrence state bottom **811.13px** under wider metrics. |

Per-screen follow-up audits: `data/devscratch/r6-s8/S8-01/{machines,equipment-model,fault-history}/phone-density-audit.md`. Before and targeted-after pairs are preserved under each screen's `browser/hyper-r1-phone-before/` and `browser/hyper-r1-phone-targeted/`. Native-size phone originals plus `phone-replay/phone-before-after-{light,dark}.jpg` were inspected. Final viewport/full-page matrix sheets were inspected at every requested width, light and dark; relevant phone originals were also inspected at native size. The reference and source/availability qualifications remain readable; the native host chrome limitations previously documented in section 8 remain outside this change.

### Files changed in this follow-up

- `src/frontend/src/pages/assets/operations.module.css`
- `src/frontend/src/pages/assets/locations/LocationWorkspace.tsx`
- `src/frontend/src/pages/assets/locations/MachinePageSummary.tsx`

The new design regression runs with locally available Arial/sans-serif glyphs, 0.2px tracking and 1.65 default line-height. It checks the complete phone machine card, initial model content, first phone alarm state, desktop component-list position and opened resolved state, in both schemes. It also checks no page overflow and no visible caveat below 14px. This is Linux layout stress evidence; **macOS replay remains for the coordinator**.

### Checks and exact commands

All exact argv, cwd, exit code, counts and full log paths for this follow-up, including exploratory failures, are in `data/devscratch/r6-s8/S8-01/phone-replay/commands.md` and `commands.json`; full history remains in `commands.jsonl` and `hyper-r1/commands.md`. Setup and cleanup commands are in `phone-replay/setup.md`. No fixture check is described as deployment or operator acceptance.

Final full browser command, from `src/frontend`:

```sh
env -i PATH="$PATH" HOME="$HOME" AIMMS_HARNESS_PORT=15209 AIMMS_BASE_SPRINT=s8 AIMMS_CAPTURE_BATCH=hyper-r1-phone-final node node_modules/@playwright/test/cli.js test --config=../../data/devscratch/r6-s8/S8-01/dev-browser.config.mts --reporter=list
```

**186 passed / 88 intentional project/matrix skips / 0 failed**. Linux Chromium in the task-owned `r6-design-browser-15219`; Vite **DEV**, `vite.s0.config.ts`, not a bundle/preview. Real ThemeContext, Layout, MachineIndex and MachineDetail; intercepted synthetic APIs; no login/database. Matrix specs resize from the desktop project and intentionally skip duplicate phone-project execution; focus sheets likewise run once at desktop, and two new machine-card cases apply only to phone.

Other checks actually run:

- `node node_modules/typescript/bin/tsc --noEmit -p <project>` for **all nine** projects: default plus s0/s1/s2/s3/s4/s6/s7/s8 — passed, zero final errors. The first S8 type checks found the new scheme variable widened to string; `as const` fixes it. The failure logs and passing final replay remain recorded.
- `node node_modules/vitest/vitest.mjs run` — **651 tests / 66 files passed**.
- `prek run --files <all 84 changed files>` on the host — **9 hooks passed / 9 not-applicable skips**. Scoped formatter runs that changed files in place were followed by passing replays. The final report also received its own host prek check.
- Targeted before matrix: **6 passed**; targeted after matrix: **6 passed**; isolated wider-font regressions: **10 passed / 2 intentional desktop-card skips**, later all repeated in the full passing S8 run. The first anchored grep discovered no tests; the corrected grep passed. The initial wider-font test incorrectly targeted Mantine's hidden radio input (4 passed / 2 locator failures); it now clicks the visible Cutaway label, as the existing suite does. Neither an assertion nor a timeout was weakened.
- `git diff --check` and final capture/source integrity — passed. Previously selected **600 PNGs remain unchanged**. This follow-up did not change backend code or run Django; the earlier offline backend checks remain historical evidence. Coordinator-provided S0/S2/S3 host replay counts are likewise historical, not reruns in this phone-spacing follow-up.

### Final captures and remaining gates

The current selection is **620 inspected PNGs**: 520 state/focus images (including all four viewports and light/dark states), 64 baseline viewport/full-page images, 16 marker crops, and 20 wider-font viewport/full-page images. The final selection, SHA256 hashes and inspection records are `phone-replay/final-capture-manifest.json`; the complete path list is `phone-replay/final-capture-paths.md`. Inspection sheets: `phone-replay/contact-sheets/hyper-r1-phone-final/` (62), `phone-replay/routine-sheets/` (8), `phone-replay/marker-sheet.jpg`, and `phone-replay/font-sheet{,-full}.jpg`. Metadata audits report zero failures. Final metrics: `phone-replay/fold-metrics.json` and `font-density-metrics.json`.

The previous 600-image DEV selection is historical and remains untouched. No fixture is live plant data. Missing communication status, physical geometry/applicability and absent plant timezone remain explicitly unavailable/unverified as previously documented; no new physical facts were inferred. No database access, migrations, downloads, git writes or shared-ledger edits occurred. Everything remains uncommitted for coordinator review.

Open: coordinator macOS Chromium replay and O22 Hyperagent rescoring (both reviewers >=95), followed by owner/product acceptance. This sandbox verification uses the mandated Linux browser container and does not claim those gates. CI should run these S8 specs against Vite DEV on Linux and macOS, preserve their existing fold guards, include the new wider-font cases, and export captures only with a unique explicit batch name.

## Segmented labels and remaining macOS phone fold follow-up

Scope: the coordinator's four remaining host failures after the previous phone pass. These are fixture checks in the real app theme/tab rail, not live-plant acceptance. Changes remain uncommitted.

- **Equipment label contrast:** both Equipment SegmentedControls receive the scoped `viewModeLabel` class through `classNames.label`. Inactive labels use Mantine gray-8 in light mode and dark-0 in dark mode; active labels use white on the existing primary-color shade 7. `transitionDuration={0}` makes the label and floating indicator switch together, eliminating intermediate gray/white colors during selection. There is no panel-wide color override. The wider-font design tests select Exploded, Assembled and Cutaway and compute each label's contrast immediately after each change, then check the Equipment view selector labels too.
- **Resolved phone fold:** only the FaultOccurrences outer stack uses `phoneOccurrences`, with a 3px gap at phone widths instead of 6px. The three gaps above the first card recover 9px (847.5px from the host replay would become approximately 838.5px with otherwise identical geometry). This estimate is not a macOS measurement. Caveats, font sizes, card content and desktop spacing are unchanged. The original <=844px assertion remains; an additional <=840px assertion requires at least 4px headroom for the resolved phone stress case.

Files for this follow-up: `src/frontend/src/pages/assets/equipment/EquipmentViewPanel.tsx`, `src/frontend/src/pages/assets/equipment/equipment.module.css`, `src/frontend/src/pages/assets/operations.module.css`, `src/frontend/src/pages/assets/faults/FaultOccurrences.tsx`, `src/frontend/tests/s8/pui_design.spec.ts`, and this report. Existing broader S8-01 changes remain in place.


Checks actually run for this narrow follow-up (full commands, cwd and logs in `data/devscratch/r6-s8/S8-01/label-fold-replay/commands.md`):

| Check | Actual result |
| --- | --- |
